id="infoSection"><h2>Status:</h2><p class="intro"> This is the original version (as it was originally made). This item of legislation is currently only available in its original format.</p></div><div id="viewLegContents" xml:lang="en"><div class="LegSnippet hasBlockAmends" id="viewLegSnippet"><div class="LegClearFix LegPrelims"> <p class="LegCorrectionText">This Statutory Instrument has been published to correct errors in <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2025/504">S.I. 2025/504</a> and is being issued free of charge to all known recipients of that Statutory Instrument.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegDraftText">Regulations made by the Secretary of State, laid before Parliament under section 55(3) of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a>, for approval by resolution of each House of Parliament within twenty-eight days beginning with the day on which the instrument is made, subject to extension for periods of dissolution or prorogation or during which both Houses are adjourned for more than four days.&#xD;</p> <p class="LegBanner">Statutory Instruments</p><h1 class="LegNo">2026 No. 543</h1><p class="LegSubject">SANCTIONS</p><h1 class="LegTitle">The Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) (Amendment) Regulations 2026</h1><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Made</p><p class="LegDateDate">18th May 2026</p></div><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Laid before Parliament</p><p class="LegDateDate">19th May 2026</p></div><div class="LegDate"><p class="LegDateText">Coming into force</p><p class="LegDateDate">20th May 2026</p></div><a class="LegAnchorID" id="Legislation-Preamble"></a><div class="LegEnactingText"><p class="LegText">The Secretary of State(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00001" title="Go to footnote 1" id="Backf00001">1</a>), considering the condition in section 45(2) of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act 2018(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00002" title="Go to footnote 2" id="Backf00002">2</a>) is met, makes the following Regulations in exercise of the powers conferred by sections 1, 3(1)(b)(ii) and (d)(ii), 5, 7(1)(c)(iii) and (e)(iii), 9(2)(a), 10(2), 14(1), (6)(a) and (8), 15, 17, 19, 20, 21(1), 45(1)(b), 54(2) of, and paragraphs 2(b), 3(a), (b) and (e), 4(b), (c) and (d), 6(a)(ii) and (b), 7, 11(a)(ii), 13(b), (c), (f), (h), (i), (m), (n), (q) and (w), 14(a), (f), (j) and (k), 17(d) and 20 of Schedule 1 to, that Act.&#xD;</p></div></div><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Citation, commencement and extent</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-1">1.</span>—(1) These Regulations may be cited as the Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) (Amendment) Regulations 2026.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) These Regulations come into force on 20th May 2026.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) These Regulations extend to England and Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland.&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of the Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-2">2.</span>  The Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00003" title="Go to footnote 3" id="Backf00003">3</a>) are amended in accordance with regulations 3 to 29.&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of regulation 2</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-3">3.</span>  In regulation 2 (interpretation), in the appropriate places insert—&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">detained transport asset licence</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a licence under regulation 65ZA;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">ships licence</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a licence under regulation 65ZB;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of regulation 3</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-4">4.</span>—(1) Regulation 3 (application of prohibitions and requirements outside the United Kingdom) is amended as follows.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In paragraph (3)(e), for “or a trade licence.” substitute <span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“, a trade licence, a detained transport asset licence or a ships licence.”</span></span>.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In paragraph (6)(b), for “or a trade licence,” substitute <span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“, a trade licence, a detained transport asset licence or a ships licence,”</span></span>.&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of regulation 5</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-5">5.</span>—(1) Regulation 5 (power to designate persons) is amended as follows.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In paragraph (1), after sub-paragraph (bza), insert—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(bzaa)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 46Z39 (detained transport assets);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In paragraph (1A), after sub-paragraph (d), insert—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(dza)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 46Z39 (detained transport assets);<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of regulation 21</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-6">6.</span>—(1) Regulation 21(1) (interpretation of this Part) is amended as follows.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In the definition of <span class="LegAmendingText">“defence and security goods”</span>—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (b), omit <span class="LegAmendingText">“and”</span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (c), after <span class="LegAmendingText">“biological weapons”</span> insert <span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“, and”</span></span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-6-2-c">(c)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">after paragraph (c), insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(d)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">engineering biology - ancillary goods;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) After the definition of <span class="LegAmendingText">“energy-related technology”</span>, insert—&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">engineering biology - ancillary goods</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means any thing specified in Part 5 of Schedule 3C;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of Chapter 4A of Part 5</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-7">7.</span>—(1) Chapter 4A of Part 5 (aircraft and ships) is amended as follows.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) Before regulation 46A insert—&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation of this Chapter</span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">46ZA.</span></span>  <span class="LegAmendingText">In this Chapter—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">aircraft</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes unmanned aircraft and aircraft capable of spaceflight activities;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">chartering services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the chartering of a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">crew services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the manning of a ship or the provision of a crew or members of a crew for a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">operating services</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means services associated with the operation of a ship;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">ship</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes every description of vessel (including a hovercraft) used in navigation.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In regulation 46A, for paragraph (5) substitute—&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation a </span><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“designated person”</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText"> means a person designated under regulation 5 (power to designate) for the purposes of this regulation.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(4) After regulation 46A insert—&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend2"><span LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">technical LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly provide services relating to the acquisition, sale, transfer or supply of a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraphs (1) and (2) are subject to Part 7 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) or (2) commits an offence, but—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to which the assistance, services or funds (as the case may be) were provided was a specified ship;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (2) to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to whose acquisition, sale, transfer or supply the services were provided was a specified ship.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation a </span><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“specified ship”</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText"> means a ship specified by the Secretary of State under regulation 57F (specification of ships) for the purposes of this regulation.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Procurement of services relating to specified ships</span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">46AB.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly procure services relating to a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of this regulation, services relating to a specified ship include, in particular, services involving the use of that specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 7 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with the offence of contravening paragraph (1) to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the ship in relation to which the services were procured was a specified ship.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this regulation a </span><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“specified ship”</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText"> means a ship specified by the Secretary of State under regulation 57F (specification of ships) for the purposes of this regulation.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Insertion of Chapter 4IB in Part 5</h2><p no reasonable cause to suspect that the brokering services were provided in relation to an arrangement mentioned in that paragraph.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Insertion of Chapter 4LA in Part 5</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-10">10.</span>  After regulation 46Z29 insert—&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearChapter"></div><h3 class="LegChapterFirst"><span class="LegChapterNo LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>Chapter 4LA</span></span><span class="LegChapterTitle LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Maritime transportation of liquefied natural gas</span></span></h3><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitleFirst LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Interpretation</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">46Z29A.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">In this Chapter—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">liquefied natural gas</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means liquefied natural gas—</span>&#xD;</p><div><div class="LegAlphaList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(a)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">falling within commodity code 2711 11 00, and</span>&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="LegLevel4NoAmend LegListItemNo">(b)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">which originates in or is consigned from Russia;</span>&#xD;</p></div></div></div><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">ship</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” includes every description of vessel (including a hovercraft) used in navigation, except the naval, military or air-force ships of any country;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel3Amend"><span class="LegAmendingText">“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">third country</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">” means a country that is not the United Kingdom, the Isle of Man or Russia.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph 1 of Schedule 3 applies for the purposes of interpreting the definition of liquefied natural gas in paragraph (1).</span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Maritime transportation of liquefied natural gas to and between third countries</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">46Z29B.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly, supply or deliver by ship, liquefied natural gas—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from a place in Russia to a third country, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from one third country to another third country.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of paragraph (1)—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“supply or deliver by ship”</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText"> includes any transfer of the goods concerned between ships on which those goods are being supplied or delivered as specified in that paragraph;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">a person supplying or delivering the goods concerned by ship includes a person who owns, controls, charters or operates a ship—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">on which those goods are being carried, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from or to which those goods are being transferred.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">For the purposes of paragraph (2)(b), whether a person—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">owns a ship is to be determined in accordance with regulation 57I(1)(a);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">controls a ship is to be determined in accordance with regulation 57I(1)(b).</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(4)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Regulation 57I(2) does not apply for the purposes of paragraph (3)(b).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(5)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 7 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(6)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening that prohibition to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the supply or delivery related to liquefied natural gas—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">which fell within commodity code 2711 11 00, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">which was consigned from or originated in Russia.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Financial services and funds relating to maritime transportation of liquefied natural gas</span></h4><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">46Z29C.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person must not directly or indirectly, provide financial services or funds in pursuance of or in connection with an arrangement whose object or effect is the supply or delivery by ship of liquefied natural gas—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">from one third country to another third country.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) is subject to Part 7 (Exceptions and licences).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who contravenes a prohibition in paragraph (1) commits an offence, but it is a defence for a person charged with an offence of contravening that prohibition to show that the person did not know and had no reasonable cause to suspect that the financial services or funds (as the case may be) were provided in pursuance of or in connection with the supply or delivery of liquefied natural gas—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">which was consigned from or originated in Russia.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h4 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Brokering an arrangement described in that paragraph.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Insertion of Chapter 4O in Part 5</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span or procured relating to specified ships</span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">61ZB.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">The prohibitions specified in paragraph (2) are not contravened by conduct that is necessary to protect or avoid endangering—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the safety of any ship, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the life of any person.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">Paragraph (1) applies to the prohibitions—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in regulation 46AA (provision of services relating to specified ships);</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in regulation 46AB (procurement of services relating to specified ships).</span></span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend2"><span class="LegAmendingText">Ships: exceptions from prohibition on operating specified ships</span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">61ZC.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person does not contravene the prohibition in regulation 57ZA(2) (operating specified ships) by conduct in the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea where the person—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">is not a United Kingdom person, and</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the person’s presence in the United Kingdom or in the territorial sea arises directly from an exercise of either—</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(i)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the right of innocent passage, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP4Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP4NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(ii)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP4TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the right of transit passage,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">in accordance with the provisions of the United Nations Convention on the Law of the Sea</span>(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00016" title="Go to footnote 16" id="Backf00016">16</a>)<span class="LegAmendingText">.</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person does not contravene the prohibition in regulation 57ZA(2) by conduct necessary to protect or avoid endangering—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the safety of any ship, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">the life of any person.<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>.</span></span>&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of regulation 65</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span transport asset: licensing offences</span></h3><p class="LegP1ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegP1No"><span class="LegAmendingText">68ZA.</span></span>—<span class="LegAmendingText">(1)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person (“</span><span class="LegTerm"><span class="LegAmendingText">P</span></span><span class="LegAmendingText">”) commits an offence if P knowingly or recklessly—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(a)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides information that is false in a material respect, or</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(b)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">provides or produces a document that is not what it purports to be,</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">for the purpose of obtaining a detained transport asset licence (whether for P or anyone else).</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(2)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A person who purports to act under the authority of a detained transport asset licence but who fails to comply with any condition of the licence commits an offence.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText LegAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">(3)</span> <span class="LegAmendingText">A licence in respect of which an offence under paragraph (1) has been committed is to be treated as void from the time at which it was issued.</span>&#xD;</p><h3 class="LegP1GroupTitle LegAmend2"><span in Schedule 4 to these Regulations.&#xD;</p><h2 class="LegP1GroupTitle">Amendment of the Trade, Aircraft and Shipping Sanctions (Civil Enforcement) Regulations 2024</h2><p class="LegP1ParaText"><span class="LegP1No" id="regulation-30">30.</span>—(1) The Trade, Aircraft and Shipping Sanctions (Civil Enforcement) Regulations 2024(<a class="LegFootnoteRef" href="#f00017" title="Go to footnote 17" id="Backf00017">17</a>) are amended as follows.&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(2) In regulation 26(3) (information offences relating to trade sanctions: application of CEMA)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-30-2-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">at the end of sub-paragraph (a), insert <span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“except where the offence relates to regulation 46Z39 (detained transport assets) and regulation 68ZA (detained transport assets: licensing offences) of the Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019”</span></span>;</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-30-2-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">at the end of sub-paragraph (b), insert <span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“except regulation 46Z39 (detained transport assets) and regulation 68ZA (detained transport assets: licensing offences) of the Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019”</span></span>.</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegP2ParaText">(3) In regulation 30 (aircraft sanctions regulations and shipping sanctions regulations: application of Chapter 1 of Part 2 of Serious Organised Crime and Police Act 2005)—&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-30-3-a">(a)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">before paragraph (a) insert—</span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container LegAmend"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3NoAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendQuote">“</span>(za)</span></span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3TextAmend"><span class="LegAmendingText">regulation 46Z39 (detained transport assets), regulation 55(2), (but only in so far as the offence under that regulation relates to circumvention of the prohibitions in regulation 46Z39 (detained transport assets)) or regulation 68ZA (detained transport assets: licensing offences) of the Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019;<span class="LegAmendQuote">”</span>;</span></span>&#xD;</p><p class="LegClearFix LegP3Container"><span class="LegDS LegLHS LegP3No" id="regulation-30-3-b">(b)</span><span class="LegDS LegRHS LegP3Text">in paragraph (a), after <span class="LegAmendingText">“6A (aircraft),”</span>, insert <span class="LegAmendingText"><span class="LegAmendingText">“regulation 68ZB (ships: licensing offences),”</span></span>.</span>&#xD;</p><div class="LegClearFix LegSignedSection"><div class="LegClearFix LegSignatory"><div class="LegClearFix LegSignee"><p class="LegPersonName">Stephen class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">2921 21;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">2921 51 90;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3204 17;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3206 11;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3402 39 90;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3402 42;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3404 90;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3802 10;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">3913 90;&#xD;</p></li><li><p class="LegListTextStandard LegLevel4">6909 11.&#xD;</p></li></ul><a class="LegAnchorID" id="Legislation-ExNote"></a><h2 class="LegExpNoteTitle">Explanatory Note</h2><p class="LegCommentText">(This note is not part of the Regulations)&#xD;</p> <p class="LegExpNoteText">These Regulations are made under the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a>. Regulations 3 to 29 amend the Russia (Sanctions) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019 (<a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2019/855">S.I. 2019/855</a>) (“<span class="LegTerm">the 2019 Regulations</span>”). Regulation 30 amends the Trade, Aircraft and Shipping Sanctions (Civil Enforcement) Regulations 2024 (<a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2024/948">S.I. 2024/948</a>) (“<span class="LegTerm">the TASSCE Regulations</span>”).&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">These Regulations amend Part 5 (Trade), and associated Schedules, of the 2019 Regulations to impose new trade sanctions—&#xD;</p> <div><div class="LegRomanList"><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(i)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">in relation to the provision and procurement of services relating to specified ships (regulation 7);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(ii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">in relation to the import and provision of ancillary services relating to relevant processed oil products (regulation 8);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(iii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">in relation to the import, acquisition, supply and delivery and provision of ancillary services relating to uranium (regulation 9);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(iv)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">in relation to the maritime transportation and provision of ancillary services relating to liquefied natural gas (regulation 10);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(v)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">in relation to the acquisition of a detained transport asset (regulation 11). Consequential amendments are made at regulations regulation 3 and 4. Regulation 5 amends regulation 5 of the 2019 Regulations to specify the prohibition on acquisition of a detained transport asset as a purpose for which a person may be designated;&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(vi)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">in relation to the provision of construction services (regulation 12);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(vii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">to add additional goods to Schedule 2A (critical industry goods and critical industry technology) (regulation 29);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(viii)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">to add additional goods to Schedule 2E (quantum computing and advanced materials technology) (regulation 29);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(ix)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">to add additional goods to Schedule 3C (defence and security goods and defence and security technology) (regulations 6 and 29);&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegListItem"><div class="ListItemUnknownNo LegListItemNo">(x)</div><p class="LegListTextStandard ListItemUnknown">to add additional goods to Schedule 3E (G7 dependency and further goods and G7 dependency and further technology) (regulation 29).&#xD;</p></div></div></div>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">Regulations 13 to 15 amend Part 6 (Ships) of the 2019 Regulations to impose new shipping sanctions in relation to specified ships, to specify the new trade and shipping sanctions as purposes for which a ship may be specified and to specify additional relevant activities a ship may be involved in. Consequential amendments are made at regulations regulation 3 and 4.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">Regulations 16 to 19 create exceptions to the prohibitions introduced. Regulations 20 to 22 make provision for licences and regulation 23 creates licensing offences. Regulations 25 to 28 make provision for the enforcement of the new prohibitions. Regulation 24 contains consequential amendments to information powers.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">Regulation 29 also amends Schedules to the 2019 Regulations to correct commodity codes and descriptions, omit commodity codes that have been replaced at a higher level and introduce definitions relating to the uranium and construction services prohibitions.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">Regulation 30 amends the TASSCE Regulations to carve offences related to the acquisition of a detained transport asset out from CEMA enforcement and to bring ship licensing offences and certain offences related to the acquisition of a detained transport asset within scope of the investigatory powers in Chapter 1 of Part 2 of the Serious Organised Crime and Police Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2005/15">2005 (c. 15)</a>.&#xD;</p>  <p class="LegExpNoteText">A full impact assessment of the effect that this instrument will have on the costs of business, the voluntary sector and the public sector is available from the Foreign, Commonwealth and Development Office, King Charles Street, London, SW1A 2AH.&#xD;</p> <div class="LegFootnotes"><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnotesContainer"><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00001"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">1</span>)</div><p class="LegText">The power to make regulations under Part 1 of the Sanctions and Anti-Money Laundering Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 (c. 13)</a> is conferred on an appropriate Minister. Section 1(9)(a) of the Act defines an “appropriate Minister” as including the Secretary of State.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00002"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">2</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2018/13">2018 c. 13</a>. Sections 1(5) and 15 are amended by the Economic Crime and Corporate Transparency Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2023/56">2023 (c. 56)</a> (“<span class="LegTerm" id="term-the-2023-act">the 2023 Act</span>”), section 35(2) and (5). Section 17 is amended by the Sentencing Act <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/ukpga/2020/17">2020 (c. 17)</a>, Schedule 24, paragraph 443(1), the 2023 Act, section 214(3) and by <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2022/500">S.I. 2022/500</a> and <a class="LegCitation" title="Go to item of legislation" rel="cite" href="/id/uksi/2023/149">S.I. 2023/149</a>. sections 57 and 62.&#xD;</p></div><div class="LegClearFix LegFootnote" id="f00003"><div class="LegFootnoteNoContainer">(<span class="LegFootnoteNo">3</span>)</div><p class="LegText"><a Changes we have not yet applied to the text, can be found in the ‘Changes to Legislation’ area.</p><p><strong>Original (As Enacted or Made): </strong>The original version of the legislation as it stood when it was enacted or made. No changes have been applied to the text.</p></div></div><div class="help" id="openingOptionsHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>Opening Options</h3><p>Different options to open legislation in order to view more content on screen at once</p></div></div><div class="help" id="emTabHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>Explanatory Memorandum</h3><p>Explanatory Memorandum sets out a brief statement of the purpose of a Statutory Instrument and provides information about its policy objective and policy implications. They aim to make the Statutory Instrument accessible to readers who are not legally qualified and accompany any Statutory Instrument or Draft Statutory Instrument laid before Parliament from June 2004 onwards.</p></div></div><div class="help" id="moreResourcesTabHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>More Resources</h3><p>Access essential accompanying documents and information for this legislation item from this tab. Dependent on the legislation item being viewed this may include:</p><ul><li>the original print PDF of the as enacted version that was used for the print copy</li><li>lists of changes made by and/or affecting this legislation item</li><li>confers power and blanket amendment details</li><li>all formats of all associated documents</li><li>correction slips</li><li>links to related legislation and further information resources</li></ul></div></div><div class="help" id="moreIATabHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>Impact Assessments</h3><p>Impact Assessments generally accompany all UK Government interventions of a regulatory nature that affect the private sector, civil society organisations and public services. They apply regardless of whether the regulation originates from a domestic or international source and can accompany primary (Acts etc) and secondary legislation (SIs). An Impact Assessment allows those with an interest in the policy area to understand:</p><ul><li>Why the government is proposing to intervene;</li><li>The main options the government is considering, and which one is preferred;</li><li>How and to what extent new policies may impact on them; and,</li><li>The estimated costs and benefits of proposed measures.</li></ul></div></div><div class="help" id="moreResourcesHelp"><span class="icon"></span><div class="content"><a href="#" class="close"><img alt="Close" src="/images/chrome/closeIcon.gif" /></a><h3>More Resources</h3><p>Use this menu to access essential accompanying documents and information for this legislation item.
